# memory.md — shared state for the main agent and the reviewer

This file is the **only** channel between the main agent (builds) and the reviewer subagent (verifies, merges). A new session must be able to continue from this file alone. Keep it current: update it **before** handing off, and after every state change.

Repo: Matt-Murungi/home-school · Board: https://github.com/users/Matt-Murungi/projects/8 · Backlog IDs (D01, F03, M05…) are in each issue's first line.
Product docs: `/Users/mat/Library/Mobile Documents/com~apple~CloudDocs/Work Space/Projects/Home School/` (System Requirements v0.2 is the stack authority; stack is **Appwrite**, not Supabase).

## Owner decisions (24–25 Nov 2026) — binding

1. **Workflow:** main agent builds one ticket (tests first, then code), updates this file, hands to reviewer. Reviewer runs the tests, checks every acceptance criterion and edge case against the issue, then either sends it back with findings or opens a PR, reviews it, and merges it. Owner is consulted only at the end.
2. **Appwrite schema:** the live project already has 35 tables not in this repo. Build the schema fresh from the tickets and System Requirements §5, then record the **diff against the live project** in the section "Questions for the owner". Do not assume the live schema is right.
3. **Live Appwrite project is READ-ONLY.** Never create, update or delete anything there. Tests run offline against stand-ins. Anything that needs a real deployment goes in "Blocked on owner".
4. **Blocked work:** for tickets that need the owner's accounts, devices or decisions (Fly.io deploy, MoMo sandbox/credentials, card gateway, email domain, app stores, Codemagic, lawyer/DPO), build the foundation (interface/adapter, tests, docs), merge it, and list exactly what is missing in "Blocked on owner" and "Questions for the owner". Do not deploy, do not pick the provider.
5. **Stop condition:** when every ticket is merged or blocked, stop and bring the owner the consolidated "Questions for the owner" list.

## Rules for both agents

- One ticket = one branch `<id>-<slug>` (e.g. `f03-core-service`) = one PR into `main`. PR title starts with the backlog ID. PR body links the issue (`Closes #NN`) and ends with the line `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Never print, log or commit secrets. Credentials live in `/Users/mat/Workspace/.workspace_env` (never copy values into the repo or this file). `.env*` is git-ignored except `.env.example`.
- Do ticket order by "Blocked by" links in the issue. Never start a ticket whose blockers aren't merged (or marked blocked-foundation-merged).
- Tests must pass from a clean checkout using documented commands. Record the exact command per package under "How to run tests".
- Money is integer UGX. Timestamps stored UTC, shown in Africa/Kampala. Appwrite caps list totals at 5,000 (ADR 0004). Appwrite has no conditional update, triggers or exclusion constraints (ADRs 0002, 0012).
- Update the GitHub issue checkboxes and move nothing on the board by hand: the auto-add workflow handles the board; reviewer closes the issue by merging with `Closes #NN`.

## Hand-off protocol

**memory.md is committed straight to `main` (message `memory: …`), never inside a ticket PR.** This avoids merge conflicts. Always `git pull` before editing it, commit and push right after. Only one agent works at a time; the main agent waits for the reviewer's verdict before starting the next ticket.
The reviewer verifies in a separate worktree (`git worktree add <scratchpad>/review-<id> <branch>`), never in the main checkout, and removes it afterwards. A PR cannot be approved by its own author on GitHub, so the reviewer posts its review with `gh pr review --comment` and merges with `gh pr merge --squash --delete-branch`.

Each ticket has a block under "Ticket log" with a `Status`:
`todo` → `in-progress` (main) → `ready-for-review` (main) → `changes-requested` (reviewer) → `ready-for-review` … → `merged` (reviewer) | `blocked-foundation-merged`.
Main agent writes: what was built, files, test command + result, which acceptance criteria/edge cases map to which tests, deviations, open questions.
Reviewer writes: commands run, results, criterion-by-criterion verdict, findings, PR number, merge commit.

## Builder checklist (main agent role; a builder subagent follows this too)

1. `git pull` main; read this file; read the issue (`gh issue view N -R Matt-Murungi/home-school`) and the SR/PRD sections its "Spec refs" cite (product docs folder above). Confirm all "Blocked by" tickets are `merged` or `blocked-foundation-merged`; if not, stop and report.
2. Branch `<id>-<slug>` from fresh main. Work in a git worktree under the scratchpad if another agent could touch the main checkout.
3. **Tests first**: write tests that encode each acceptance criterion and each edge case in the issue; see them fail; then write the code until they pass. Keep tests runnable offline (no live Appwrite, no network, no real providers). Use stand-ins/fakes behind interfaces (adapter pattern) for Appwrite, MoMo, FCM, email, SMS, Maps, Sentry.
4. Put new packages where F01 (#27) defines: `apps/parent`, `apps/teacher`, `apps/admin`, `packages/flutter_shared`, `services/core`, `appwrite/`, `docs/`. Add each package's test command to "How to run tests". Keep the diff to one ticket.
5. Anything needing the owner's account, credentials, deployment or a product decision: build the interface, a fake implementation and tests, document the missing piece, add it to "Blocked on owner" and "Questions for the owner" (with your recommended default), and mark the ticket `blocked-foundation-merged` after merge. Never deploy, never call live services, never pick the payment gateway or email domain.
6. Commit on the branch (message starts with the backlog ID, ends with the Co-Authored-By line), push the branch. **Do not open the PR** (the reviewer does).
7. Update this file on main (`memory: ...` commit): ticket block with Status `ready-for-review`, what was built, files, test command and result, a table mapping each acceptance criterion and edge case to a test (or to "owner/blocked" with why), deviations, follow-ups. Enough detail for a fresh session to continue.
8. Report briefly: what was built, test result, anything blocked.

## Reviewer checklist

1. `git pull` main; read this file and the ticket block; read the issue.
2. Verify in a separate worktree: run the documented test commands from a clean checkout (record the real output); try to break the new logic with your own repro scripts; check every acceptance criterion and edge case against code and tests (a criterion without a test that proves it is a finding); check the diff for secrets, accidental live-service calls, committed `.env`, and that the diff is only this ticket.
3. **Reviewers do not fix code** (only trivial typos/formatting). Independence matters: code fixed by its own reviewer is unreviewed. If anything is amiss: ticket Status `changes-requested` with precise findings, commit to main, stop and report.
4. If fine: open PR (title starts with backlog ID; body: summary, evidence, caveats, `Closes #N`, the 🤖 line), review the diff, `gh pr review --comment`, `gh pr merge --squash --delete-branch`, confirm the issue closed, set Status `merged` (or `blocked-foundation-merged` for owner-blocked tickets) with PR number and commands/results, commit and push memory.md to main.
5. Report: verdict, commands and results, findings, PR URL.

### Reviewer setup: exact commands to get a green baseline

Clean checkout or fresh `git worktree` (needs branch `chore-main-health` merged for `make setup`; details and troubleshooting in CONTRIBUTING.md). Run one at a time; the machine is often busy.
```
export npm_config_cache=/tmp/hs-npm-cache   # ABSOLUTE path; only if ~/.npm has root-owned files (EACCES)
make setup                                   # npm ci in apps/admin
make lint
make test
python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests
uvx pre-commit run --all-files               # optional
```
Failures such as `eslint: command not found`, `apps/admin needs npm ci first` or an npx download mean setup was skipped, not a repo bug. A failure may be called environment noise only with the command output that proves it.

## How to run tests

| Package | Command |
|---|---|
| spikes/admin-appwrite | `cd spikes/admin-appwrite && npm ci && npx tsc --noEmit && npm run lint && npm run build` |
| spikes/jobs-demo | `cd spikes/jobs-demo && uv run pytest -q` |
| packages/flutter_shared | `cd packages/flutter_shared && flutter test` (lint: `dart format --set-exit-if-changed . && flutter analyze`) |
| apps/parent | `cd apps/parent && flutter test` (same lint) |
| apps/teacher | `cd apps/teacher && flutter test` (same lint) |
| ops (F08) | `cd ops && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests && python3 check_ops.py` |
| services/core (F03) | `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q` (lint: `uv run ruff check . && uv run ruff format --check .`); offline, no Docker needed |
| docs/legal (policy inventory) | `python3 docs/legal/check-policy-inventory.py` (fails on missing purpose/consumer ticket/owner/status or unknown ticket #) |
| docs/adr (ADR lint; reuse for every decision ticket) | `python3 docs/adr/check-adrs.py` (all ADRs) or `python3 docs/adr/check-adrs.py docs/adr/00NN-x.md`. Needs `# ADR NNNN — title` (number = file name), `- **Status:**` (Proposed/Accepted/Superseded/Deprecated), `- **Date:**`, `- **Ticket:**`, and `## Context`, `## Decision`, `## Consequences`. Exit 1 on failure. Stdlib only. |
| services/core jobs + function (F04) | same command as services/core (`cd services/core && uv run pytest -q`, 166 tests). The `appwrite/functions/job-tick` tests run there too (`tests/test_job_tick_function.py`). Concurrency tests only: `uv run pytest -q -k "two_workers or atomic or overlapping or stalled"` (repeat 10x). |
| root tooling (F01) | `make test` (every implemented package per `packages.json`; planned ones skip with a message; `HS_STRICT=1` fails on missing tools, `HS_SKIP=id,id`) and `make lint` (layout guard + secret scan + package lint). F01 tests alone: `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests -v`. Stdlib only. |
| apps/admin (F06) | `cd apps/admin && npm ci && npx tsc --noEmit && npm test` (vitest + React Testing Library, offline, 124 tests); then `npm run lint` and `npm run build`. Root `make test` runs it via `packages.json`. If `~/.npm` has root-owned files, add `--cache <tmpdir>` to npm. Playwright not used (needs browser download). |
| appwrite (F02) | `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -v` (60 tests, stdlib only, offline). Also `python3 appwrite/check_keys.py` validates `appwrite/keys.md`. Migration runner: `python3 appwrite/migrate.py --env staging` (needs real env, owner-blocked). |

| appwrite schema (M01, M02) | `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -v` (202 tests incl. `tests/test_schema.py`, `tests/test_sessions_schema.py`); `python3 appwrite/schema_build.py --check` (validate + generated files fresh), `--write` to regenerate config and `docs/erd`; `python3 appwrite/diff_live.py <snapshot.json>` (offline live diff) |
| appwrite catalogue (M03) | same command as appwrite schema (278 tests incl. `tests/test_catalogue_schema.py`, 71); rules reference in `appwrite/catalogue_rules.py` |
| core append-only guard (M04) | `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q tests/test_append_only.py` (17 tests; AST scan of `src/hs_core` for update/delete on ledger_entry, ledger_transaction, audit_log, safeguarding_action); part of the normal core run |
| ledger ($01) | `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q tests/test_ledger_*.py` (offline; crash/fuzz/concurrency/scale/mutation; ~280 tests, ~10 s); part of the normal core run. Design: `docs/ops/ledger.md` |
| secrets (F09) | `python3 scripts/secrets_check.py` (validates `docs/ops/secrets-inventory.json`, `.env.example`, generated docs; `--write` regenerates tables), tests `python3 -m unittest scripts.tests.test_secrets` (36) and `cd services/core && uv run pytest tests/test_config_inventory.py`. Also part of `make test`/`make lint` via `root-tests`. Stdlib only, offline. |
| py_momo ($02) | `cd packages/py_momo && uv run pytest -q` (293 tests, offline, stdlib-only package) and `uv run ruff check . && uv run ruff format --check .`; registered in `packages.json` as `py-momo`. Manual sandbox smoke test (owner only, network): `python packages/py_momo/scripts/manual_sandbox.py --yes-call-the-network` |
| legal docs (C04) | `cd docs/legal && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests && python3 check-processors.py` (39 tests; validator checks `processors.json` vs `docs/ops/secrets-inventory.json` when F09 is on main, SR §9 integrations, required fields, register markdown). Stdlib, offline. Also in `make test` via `legal-docs` in packages.json. |

| CI/CD (F07) | `python3 scripts/ci_lint.py` (workflow rules) and `python3 -m unittest scripts.tests.test_workflows` (83 tests, stdlib; PyYAML cross-check via uv if online). actionlint: `uvx --from actionlint-py==1.7.12.25 actionlint .github/workflows/*.yml`. Part of `make test`/`make lint` via `root-tests`. Real verification = the PR's checks. |

| permissions (M05) | `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q tests/test_perm_*.py` (778 offline tests: role x table matrix on the permission-aware fake, rules, service failure modes, jobs, property test with an oracle); drift check `cd services/core && uv run python scripts/permissions_check.py` (also in core lint; `--write-docs` regenerates `docs/ops/permissions.md`); schema `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest tests.test_permissions_schema` |

(Add a row for each new package.)

## Ticket queue (MVP build order)

Source of truth for order and dependencies is each issue. Epics E00–E19, 135 tickets (issues #21–#155; epics #1–#20).
Suggested order: E00 decisions (D01–D03, D05; D04, D06 later/owner) → E01 foundations → E02 data model → E03 auth → E04 catalogue → E05 teacher onboarding → E06 parent onboarding → E07 plans → E08 payments → E09 matching → E10 scheduling → E11 session day → E12 logs → E13 safeguarding → E14 pay → E15 notifications → E16 admin → E17 compliance → E18 assessments → E19 quality/launch. Phase 2/3 tickets last.

Relative links in README/CONTRIBUTING/CLAUDE/package READMEs all resolve. Diff is only F01 (24 files), no secrets. CLAUDE.md/CONTRIBUTING rules match Owner decisions and ADRs 0002/0003/0004/0012 (alphanumeric labels, 5,000 cap, no conditional update/triggers/exclusion, read-only Appwrite, memory.md on main).
- **Findings (fix on the branch, re-submit):**
  1. **Pre-commit hooks fail on this repo's own files** (acceptance criterion "hooks for formatting and linting in each language"). In `.pre-commit-config.yaml` the `ruff-check` and `ruff-format` hooks use `files: \.py$` (named "services/core" but unscoped) so they run on `scripts/*.py` and `spikes/jobs-demo`. Reviewer ran `uvx ruff check scripts` -> 6 errors and `uvx ruff format --check scripts` -> 4 of 5 files would be reformatted, so any commit touching those files would be blocked once `make hooks` is installed. Fix: scope hooks to the package they name (`^services/core/.*\.py$`; dart hooks to `^(apps|packages)/.*\.dart$`), or ruff-format the scripts and fix the 6 lint errors; and note hooks were not executed (pre-commit missing).
  2. Secret scan misses JSON/quoted keys: a JSON-style quoted key with a 21-character value (e.g. a quoted `password` field) is not flagged (the `generic-assignment` regex needs `key[:=]` with no closing quote between). Allow an optional quote before the separator and add a unit test. Also the placeholder filter skips any line containing `your|example|<|process.env...` anywhere, so `secret = "<20+ chars>" # example` passes; narrow it to the value (not the whole line) or document it as a known limit in `scripts/secret_scan.py`.
  3. Minor doc: CONTRIBUTING step 7 says to open a PR; CLAUDE.md rule 9 says builders do not open PRs (reviewer does). Say "humans open the PR; agent builders hand off to the reviewer".
  Not blocking: red-before-green not captured (mutations show tests fail when code is broken); branch protection owner-blocked until F07 CI exists (acceptable); no CI (F07).
- **Round 2 resolutions (commit a4ed94b, tests written first and seen red: 10 failures, then green):**
  1. Hooks: `dart-format` now `^(apps|packages)/.*\.dart$`; `ruff-check`/`ruff-format` now `^(services/core|scripts)/.*\.py$` (spikes/ excluded with a comment, docs/ python covered by ADR lint). `scripts/*.py` made ruff-clean (explicit `check=False`, `ClassVar`, exec bit on shebang scripts, ruff format). Tests `scripts/tests/test_round2.py`: yaml-free parser asserts every `files:` is anchored, language hooks are scoped, regexes match services/core + scripts and not spikes/docs; ruff check/format tests on `scripts` (skip clearly if neither ruff nor uvx). New `make pylint` (part of `make lint`) runs ruff on scripts, skips if tool missing, fails under `HS_STRICT=1`. Real runs: `uvx ruff check scripts` -> All checks passed; `uvx ruff format --check scripts` -> 6 files already formatted. pre-commit itself still not installed here, so hooks are not executed (parsed and regex-tested only).
  2. `secret_scan.py`: optional quote before the separator (flags `{"password": "..."}`, `"api_key": "..."`, single-quoted too); placeholder filter now applies to the matched VALUE only, so `secret = "<real>" # example` is flagged. Tests: JSON keys flagged, real value + example/your/< comment flagged, placeholders not flagged (`APPWRITE_API_KEY=`, `<your-key>`, `changeme`, `xxxxxxxx`, `${VAR}`, `process.env`, `os.environ`), value never printed. Known limit: a real secret whose value itself contains `example`/`your`/`xxxx` is skipped.
  3. CONTRIBUTING step 7 now: humans open the PR; agent builders push and hand off to the reviewer (CLAUDE.md rule 9); test checks the wording.
  Results: `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests` Ran 34 OK; `make lint` exit 0 (layout ok, secret-scan ok, ADR 4/4, ruff clean); `make test` exit 0 (4 ran ok, 6 planned skipped, jobs-demo 14 passed).

Finding (1) resolved for ruff/dart: regexes match only services/core+scripts / apps+packages dart, not spikes/docs. Finding (2) resolved: planted JSON-quoted, single-quoted, `# example` suffix, YAML, `.env`, `API_TOKEN="..."`, `"secret":"..."` all flagged with value never printed; placeholders/short/env-ref lines not flagged. Finding (3) resolved. `uvx pre-commit run --all-files` DID run (no system install needed): all pass EXCEPT `prettier`.
  **New finding R2-1:** hook `prettier` (`files: ^apps/admin/.*\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|json|css|md)$`, entry `npx --no-install prettier --check`) matches the existing `apps/admin/README.md` and fails ("npx canceled due to missing packages") because apps/admin has no node package yet. So any commit touching that README is blocked: same class as round-1 finding 1. Fix: drop `md` from the prettier pattern (or make the hook skip when `apps/admin/package.json` is absent, e.g. entry wrapper that exits 0 without it, same for eslint), and extend `test_round2.py` to assert the hook does not select the placeholder README. Re-run `uvx pre-commit run --all-files` to confirm all green, then re-submit. Everything else is ready to merge.

New tests in `scripts/tests/test_round2.py`: every hook `files:` regex run against all tracked/present files under apps, packages, services, scripts selects only files of an extension the hook can process; no hook selects any `README.md`; node hooks must carry the package.json guard; the guard is executed for real in an empty dir and must exit 0; `secret_scan.py --path <dir>` CLI exits 0 clean and 1 with a planted fake AWS key (value not printed). ### F02 · Appwrite projects for dev, staging, production (#28)
- Status: **blocked-foundation-merged** (PR #163, squash; issue #28 closed). Blocked on owner: create the 3 projects/platforms, keys, secrets-manager entries; CI staging push (F07).
- Review: 60 tests, make lint, make test, scripts tests, pre-commit all green after rebase (conflicts in packages.json/README rows resolved, both sides kept). Adapter paths for tables/columns checked against the Appwrite Python SDK source; row/index paths still unverified live. Follow-ups (non-blocking): (1) `run()`/`main()` do not catch `AppwriteError` (5xx, 409, missing key env var): traceback, exit 1, no `PROMOTE=false` line, so F07 must gate on exit code and `promote == 'true'`; fix by catching and printing. (2) Seed guard is by file name only (`NNNN_seed*`): `0003_sample_seed.py` would run in production; `seedAllowed` in environments.json is not read by the runner. (3) No run lock: concurrent runs both execute pending scripts, unique version index fails the second record; F07 needs a GitHub concurrency group. (4) `ensure_migration_table` not resumable after partial creation.
- Built (all under `appwrite/`, plus packages.json entry flipped to implemented, README layout row, `.gitignore` entry for `appwrite/environments.json`): `appwrite.config.json` (CLI-format skeleton: db `main`, tables `schema_migration` + sample `sample_item`, bucket `teacher-docs`, no functions; ADR 0012 rules: rowSecurity on, empty `$permissions`, fileSecurity on); `environments.example.json` (3 envs, Frankfurt endpoint, placeholder project ids, `keyEnvVar` names the env var holding each key, `seedAllowed`); `migrate.py` (runner + `AppwriteClient` interface); `fake_client.py` (test stand-in); `appwrite_client.py` (urllib REST adapter, mocked-only, key from `os.environ` only); `migrations/0001_schema_baseline.py`; `keys.md` + `check_keys.py`; `tests/` (60 tests).
- Test command: `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -v` -> Ran 60 OK (red first: import errors, then green). `make test` runs it as package `appwrite`.
- Runner contract: scripts `NNNN_name.py` with `up(ctx)`; `NNNN_seed_*.py` are seeds; exit 0 ok / 1 failed / 2 invalid / 3 seed refused in production; always prints `PROMOTE=true|false` and writes `promote=` to `$GITHUB_OUTPUT`. Also detects edited applied scripts, duplicate versions, applied-but-missing scripts.

| Criterion / edge case | Evidence |
|---|---|
| Three projects, separate keys, buckets, platforms (Android/iOS/web) | OWNER-BLOCKED (create projects). Foundation: `test_config.Environments` (3 envs, distinct key env var each, platforms listed, placeholders only) |
| Schema applies to staging from CI, re-run no-op | `appwrite push` from CI OWNER-BLOCKED (needs staging project + F07 CI). Runner no-op: `Ordering.test_rerun_is_a_noop`, `test_new_script_after_rerun_runs_only_the_new_one`; config shape: `test_config.Config` |
| Keys scoped per consumer, fewest scopes | `test_keys` (allowlist per consumer, no wildcard, core cannot hold deploy scopes, CI cannot hold users.*, expiry, one row per consumer+env, repo `keys.md` passes). Creating the keys is OWNER-BLOCKED |
| Seed only dev/staging, never production | `SeedGuard` (production refused before anything runs, alias/case variants, already-recorded seed, CLI exit 3, unknown env) |
| Keys in secrets manager not repo | `NoSecretsCommitted` (secret_scan on appwrite/, key-like value scan, no .env files, adapter never reads key from a file); entries themselves OWNER-BLOCKED |
| Edge: push fails halfway -> CI stops, no production | `FailureStopsEverything` (stops at first failure, later scripts not run, PROMOTE=false, retried next run, CLI exit 1) |
| Edge: column/index still processing | `WaitsForAvailable` (waits and polls, script sees only available, timeout fails without running script, `failed` fails fast) |
| CI job applies sample schema change to staging | OWNER-BLOCKED (needs staging + F07). `sample_item` + `0001` are the sample |
| Secret scan finds no keys | `test_secret_scan_clean_on_appwrite_dir` (note: whole-repo scan currently fails on main at `memory.md:145`, F01 reviewer text quoting a sample password; not mine, reword that line) |
| Real adapter | `test_adapter` (mocked opener, no network). REST paths (`/tablesdb/{db}/tables/{t}/columns|indexes|rows`) written from docs, NOT run live: verify once against dev |

- Owner steps (exact): 1) In Appwrite Cloud (Frankfurt) create projects `hs-development`, `hs-staging`, `hs-production` (the existing live project may be re-used as production only after the owner decides; agents never touch it); in each add platforms Android, iOS, Web, create database `main`. 2) Create keys per `appwrite/keys.md` (two per project: core-service, ci-deploy; scopes exactly as listed; 90-day expiry). 3) Store: core keys as Fly secret `APPWRITE_API_KEY` per Fly app; CI keys as GitHub environment secrets (`development`, `staging`, `production` with required reviewers) named `APPWRITE_API_KEY`, plus project ids as environment variables; copy `environments.example.json` to a CI-generated `environments.json`. 4) Run `appwrite push` + `python3 appwrite/migrate.py --env development` once by hand and confirm the REST adapter works, then F07 wires staging. 5) Replace the "scopes to re-check" with the console's actual scope names if they differ.

Not covered: `desired_permissions` tests from ADR 0012 belong with F03/M tickets (no permissions code here).

### F08 · Error tracking, logging and uptime monitoring (#34)

- **Status:** blocked-foundation-merged (PR #169 squash-merged, round 3 reviewer 25 Nov; owner-blocked: Sentry account/DSNs, alert routing, uptime vendor, live alert proofs). Round 3 review: R2-1 fixed and re-measured with own adversarial inputs (20 shapes x 100 KB/256 KB/1 MB/10 MB through scrub_text/scrub_event/scrub_breadcrumb/JsonFormatter: <=0.1 s, doubles per doubling; 200-input fuzz 5.5 s), core pytest 529 x3, ruff, ops, make lint ok, `make test` only the 2 known test_round2 prettier failures. Non-blocking follow-up: `_mask_pairs` runs `_VALUE.match` (up to 4096 chars) before `_secret_name`, so runs of non-secret pairs (`x=x=x=...`, `a%3D`) cost ~12 us/byte (1.2 s per 100 KB, linear, capped at 256 KB per string): check the name first. `mykey=abc` is not masked (by design). F09 not merged: `SENTRY_DSN`/`NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` need inventory rows (note in docs/ops/setup.md).
- **Round 3 fixes (25 Nov), tests first in `services/core/tests/test_scrub_linear.py` (205 cases; they failed/timed out before):** R2-1: `_KV_EQ`/`_KV_JSON` and `_SECRET_NAME` removed. `scrub.py` now finds a run of name characters with `[\w.-]+` plus an optional bounded separator group (`"?`, up to 8 blanks, `=`/`:`/`%3D`), takes the last 64 characters as the name and decides in Python (`_secret_name`: parts split on `_ - .`, a part equal to/ending with token, secret, password, passwd, apikey, jwt, signature, equal to cookie/authorization/pass/pwd/session or starting `session`, `key` only as last part) so no keyword alternation exists in a regex; values are bounded (4096) and consumed so scanning resumes after them; `_BEARER`/`_HEADER_LINE` whitespace bounded. Tests: 10 keywords (token, session, secret-, passwd, api_key, apikey, password, cookie, authorization, key) x 100 KB/256 KB/1 MB through `scrub_text`, `scrub_event` (message + logentry), a real `logging` record via `JsonFormatter`, plus exception text, 10 shaped adversarial builders x 6 words, and a seeded fuzz of 40 random concatenations (up to 200 KB) with a 2 s per-input and 20 s total budget; output size asserted bounded. Non-blocking: (a) colon/space/`%3D` forms now masked (`secret: v`, `Token: v`, `password : v`, `cookie=v`, `x-appwrite-key = v`, `pass=v`, `token%3Dv`, `jwt: v`, JSON/dict-repr); NOT masked on purpose and documented in the module docstring and `docs/ops/runbook.md`: `session_count=3`, `tokens_used=5`, `password_policy=ok`, `key_count=2`, prose like `Error: token expired`; (b) phones `00256772123456`, `(0772) 123456` masked; (c) `[Filtered]]` fixed (value pattern swallows an existing `[Filtered]`); (d) `scrub_event`/`scrub_breadcrumb` drops log one `event|breadcrumb dropped by scrubber: <ExceptionClass>` warning and increment `dropped_count()` (`reset_dropped()` for tests); (e) F09 not on main yet: note added at the end of `docs/ops/setup.md` that `SENTRY_DSN` (+ `NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN`, dart-define) must be listed in the F09 inventory and its validator run after F09 merges.
- **Round 3 results:** timings (same machine) before -> after: `scrub_text('token'*20000)` 15.6 s -> 0.00 s; `'GET /'+'session'*10000` 11.1 s -> ~0.01 s; `scrub_event` on 260 KB of `token` 123 s, `session` 127 s, `api_key` 76 s -> about 0.1-0.3 s even at 1 MB (worst case in the suite 0.15 s per 100 KB log record); `scrub_text` over 256 KB of any keyword: pattern pass `_mask_pairs` 0.6-0.9 s -> 0.0 s. `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q` 529 passed x3 (205 new; whole `test_scrub_linear.py` 6 s; also green under 8 busy loops, 15 s), `ruff check` all passed, `ruff format --check` 39 files formatted, `cd ops && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` OK, `check_ops.py` ops: ok. `make test`/`make lint`: see the last line of this block. Also fixed: main's memory.md line (reviewer note) tripped `make secrets` (generic-assignment) because it quoted example secret assignments; defused to `<v>` placeholders.
- **Round 2 fixes (25 Nov), all in `observability/scrub.py` + `logging_setup.py`, tests in `services/core/tests/test_scrub_hardening.py` (written first; they hung/failed before):** (1) cycles: `_walk` tracks ids on the current path (`[Circular]`, shared acyclic refs still scrubbed), caps `MAX_NODES` 50k, `MAX_CHARS` 1M output, `MAX_TEXT` 256k per string (truncated), depth 30; cap exhaustion raises `ScrubLimitExceeded` so `scrub_event`/`scrub_breadcrumb` drop the event (fail closed). Tests: `d['self']=d;d['x']=d`, `a.append(a)` twice, breadcrumb cycle, 2**40-path DAG (dropped), 500k-wide (dropped) and 5000-wide (kept), 5000-deep, 1 MB string, 10 quadratic-regex probes at 900 KB, output-size cap. Regexes got lookbehinds so scanning is linear. (2) Free text: header lines (`Cookie:`, `Set-Cookie:`, `Authorization:`, `X-Appwrite-Key/JWT/Session`, `X-Api-Key`) masked to end of line; `standard_<hex>`; `name=value` for token/secret/password/passwd/pwd/key/`*_key`/api_key/jwt/session/signature names (so `a_session_<proj>[_legacy]=`, access_token, refresh_token, client_secret); `"name": "value"` JSON/dict-repr inside strings; percent-encoded emails (`%40`, `%2E`) and phones (`%2B256`, `%20`, `+` separators). 20 secret cases + context-kept test. (3) Phones: `+256 (0) 772 123456`, `(+256) 772 123 456`, `0772-123-456`, `256772123456` and 5 more forms x 8 punctuation templates assert exact output (no stray fragments); 15 false-positive strings (UGX 40,000, dates, hex ids, versions, 16-digit ids, ports) unchanged. (4) bytes/bytearray/memoryview values decoded `errors=replace` then scrubbed; bytes keys decoded (`b'password'` and the string `"b'token'"` masked); unknown objects stringified and scrubbed. `extra` log fields: probed, they were raw in `JsonFormatter`; now every `extra=` value goes through `scrub_data` (sensitive keys masked, text patterns, cycles/caps, fail closed to `[Filtered]`); tests `test_log_extra_*`. Sentry `extra` already went through `_walk`. Known limit unchanged: names/addresses in free text.
- **Round 2 results:** `cd services/core && uv run pytest -q` 324 passed (was 96; includes F04), `uv run ruff check .` all passed, `ruff format --check .` 38 files formatted; `cd ops && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` OK, `check_ops.py` ops: ok; `make lint` 6 ran ok / 0 failed (in the worktree needed apps/admin node_modules symlinked; not committed). Hardening tests ~2-4 s total; the timing bound is 3 s per case (loosened from 1.5 after one flake under load).
- **Round 2 reviewer (25 Nov): changes-requested, one real defect.** Rebased onto main (F05/F06 memory only), pushed `--force-with-lease` (head c08ce0f). Results: core pytest 324 passed x3, ruff ok, format ok, ops 17 OK + check_ops ok, `make lint` 9 ran ok/0 failed; hardening tests 10/10 pass under 8 busy loops (3-9 s total, so the 3 s bound is no longer flaky). Round-1 findings 1-4 verified fixed: cycles/shared/2**40 DAG/1M-wide/100k-deep/50 MB str+bytes all finish in about 1 s with ~60 MB RSS (cap exhaustion drops event); all phone formats listed in the brief masked, wa.me/tel: masked, bytes/bytearray/memoryview/bytes keys masked, `extra=`, `exc_info` and `__cause__` text masked, UGX amounts/dates/UUIDs/versions/ports/10-13 digit ids/timestamps untouched. 15 other regex probes at 900 KB (aaaa, ` +256`, digits, `%2B`, header-like, `a=`, quotes, `(0)`, bearer, emails) are linear (0.5-1.9 s).
- **BLOCKING finding R2-1: quadratic regex in `_KV_EQ`/`_KV_JSON` (`_SECRET_NAME` = `[\w.-]*(?:token|secret|passw(?:or)?d|pwd|apikey|api[_-]key|jwt|session|signature)[\w.-]*`).** On a long run of word characters that contains the keyword many times the engine backtracks to every occurrence and rescans to the end: `scrub_text('token'*20000)` (100 KB) 25 s, `'token'*40000` (200 KB) 116 s; `'GET /' + 'session'*10000` through `scrub_event` 14 s; `session`, `passwd`, `secret-`, `apikey` repeats likewise. Reachable by an attacker: `JsonFormatter.format` runs `scrub_text` on every log message and exception text, and the URL/path/header lines end up in log messages, so one request with a ~64 KB path of repeated `session` stalls a worker for ~15 s (MAX_TEXT 256k is no bound, a 256 KB string takes minutes). The round-2 quadratic tests missed it because none of the 10 probes repeat a secret keyword inside one unbroken word run. Fix suggestion: drop the leading `[\w.-]*` and trailing `[\w.-]*` greedy parts (match the keyword, then `[\w.-]*` only after it, anchored by the existing `(?<![\w.-])` lookbehind on a bounded token), or tokenise first (`re.finditer(r"[\w.-]+(?=\s*=)")` then test the name with `in`), or cap name length (`{0,64}`). Add tests with `'token'*40000`, `'session'*40000`, `'secret-'*40000`, `'passwd'*40000`, `'api_key'*40000` under the 3 s bound, for `scrub_text` and for a log record message.
- Non-blocking observations (no change required to merge after R2-1): (a) unquoted colon forms are not masked in free text: `secret: <v>`, `api_key: <v>`, `Token: <v>`, `password: <v>`; also `cookie=abc`, `x-appwrite-key = abc` (header rule needs a colon), `pass=abc`, `token%3Dabc` (encoded `=`), `jwt: eyJhbGciOiJI` (non 3-part). Bearer/JWT/`standard_`/header-line/`name=value` cover the realistic cases; document the limit or add `name: value` for the strongest names. (b) `00256772123456` and `(0772) 123456` not masked. (c) `APPWRITE_API_KEY=<standard_ key>` renders `[Filtered]]` (stray bracket, harmless). (d) Over-masking: `session_count=3`, `tokens_used=5`, `password_policy=ok` are masked (acceptable, document); 12-13 digit ids/order numbers are NOT masked (good). (e) Fail-closed drops in `scrub_event`/`scrub_breadcrumb` are silent: nothing is logged or counted, so a systematic drop (e.g. every event too large) is invisible; log one PII-free line (`log.warning("event dropped by scrubber: %s", type(exc).__name__)`) with a counter. (f) F09 `secrets_check.py` is not on main yet; when F09 merges it must list `SENTRY_DSN` (sentry_dsn setting); DSN is read only from settings, no hard-coded DSN, no transport under pytest.
- **Round 1 status line (superseded):** changes-requested.
- **Reviewer (25 Nov):** rebase onto main clean. core pytest 96 passed, ruff ok, ops unittest 17 OK + check_ops ok, `make lint` 6 ok/0 failed, pre-commit all pass. `make test` 1 failed and `scripts/tests` 2-3 failures are apps/admin tooling (tsc via npx / prettier guard test) and reproduce on pristine main, not F08. Verified OK: no hard-coded DSN, no transport under pytest (guard), 5xx reported once via `report_once`, 4xx not reported, request_id flows, log message and exc text scrubbed, alerts.json covers NFR-17, uptime hostnames come from env var names (no real hosts), ALLOWED_TOP change fine. Phones in all Ugandan formats, emails, JWT, Bearer, key masking, frame vars, request data all masked; scrub failure drops event. **Findings (all in `observability/scrub.py`, adversarial run):** (1) `_walk` is exponential on cycles with more than one back-reference: `d={}; d['self']=d; d['x']=d` or `a=[]; a.append(a); a.append(a)` hang >20 s (depth cap 30 does not bound branching), so one bad event blocks the request path and fail-closed never triggers. The SDK pre-serializes events, but `scrub_event`/`scrub_breadcrumb` are public and breadcrumbs get raw data: track visited ids (return `[Circular]`) and add a test; also cap node count. (2) Free-text secrets not masked: `a_session_<proj>=<value>` (Appwrite session cookie), `standard_<hex>` Appwrite API keys (e.g. `X-Appwrite-Key: standard_abc123`), and `token=`/`password=`/`secret=` query values in URLs (`...?phone=0772...&token=abc` leaves token). Add patterns plus tests. (3) `+256 (0) 772 123456` not masked (only the national `(0)` form); `(+256) 772 123 456` leaves a stray `(`. (4) bytes values and bytes keys pass through unscrubbed (`b'password'`); decode or drop bytes. Non-blocking: names/addresses in free text are undetectable (documented, accepted). Re-request review after fixing 1-4 with tests.
- **Built:** `services/core/src/hs_core/observability/` (`scrub.py` before_send/before_breadcrumb PII scrubber, `reporter.py` `ErrorReporter` + `SentryReporter` (own Client, sentry-sdk 3, DSN from `SENTRY_DSN` only, refuses a real transport under pytest) + `NoopReporter` + `FakeReporter` + `report_once`); wiring in `main.py`/`errors.py` (5xx reported once with request_id), `settings.py` (`sentry_dsn` SecretStr, https check), `logging_setup.py` (log message and exception text scrubbed). `ops/alerts.json`, `ops/uptime.json`, `ops/check_ops.py` (validator), new top-level dir `ops` (added to `check_layout.py` ALLOWED_TOP, `packages.json` id `ops`). Docs: `docs/ops/runbook.md`, `setup.md` (owner-blocked steps), `integration.md` (Flutter dart-define, admin, worker hooks).

Repro: `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests` (also fails at d605bb9). The reviewer note "unittest 39 OK / make test 0 failed" is wrong for the merged state. Will break F07 CI. Fix: update the hook entry or the tests deliberately (keep a skip when `apps/admin/package.json` is absent).
  - **F-2 (MEDIUM, guard test does not enumerate everything):** `tests/page-guard.test.ts` scans only `src/app/(admin)/**/page.tsx` and counts them against NAV_ITEMS. Scratch mutations, all test-green: unguarded `src/app/zzz/page.tsx` outside `(admin)`; unguarded `route.ts` in `(admin)` or under `src/app/api/`; a page whose only `requireAccess("/x")` is in a comment. (Unguarded page inside `(admin)` and a `@slot/page.tsx` do fail it, correctly.) Middleware only checks cookie presence, so any non-empty cookie reaches such a page/handler (layouts do not wrap `api/` or non-`(admin)` routes). Fix: scan all of `src/app` for page/route/default files, require a real call (regex on code, not comments) or an allow-list (`login`, `api/session`, `api/session/clear`, `forbidden`), and assert server actions (`"use server"`) call it.
  - **F-3 (LOW, unmarked nav assumption):** `nav.ts` gives `superadmin` every route (incl. Payments, Safeguarding), but SR 6.2 has no super_admin column, A-30 says "safeguarding roles only", and F03's core service decided superadmin is NOT implicit (`test_superadmin_alone_does_not_pass_opsadmin_guard`). The guess list in nav.ts names only Overview, Queues, Matching, Payouts, Reports, Settings; superadmin-all is not marked. UI would show screens whose data calls the core service refuses. Add to the UNCONFIRMED list and to Questions for the owner. Other nav entries match SR 6.2 (teachers/families/sessions ops+SG; payments ops; catalogue ops+curator; safeguarding SG; audit ops+SG).
  - **F-4 (LOW):** fresh checkout: `uvx pre-commit run --all-files` (prettier, eslint) and `make lint` (apps/admin, exit 127) fail until `npm ci` in `apps/admin` (message says so for hooks; `make lint` just exits 127, undocumented). Not fatal; document, or have `make lint` skip/clear-message when `node_modules` is missing.
  - **F-5 (LOW, follow-up accuracy):** pending-MFA cookie `maxAge` 10 min is browser-side only; the JSON has no server-checked timestamp, so a replayed cookie still works until Appwrite expires the challenge; the abandoned password-only Appwrite session is never deleted when MFA is not completed. Not exploitable without the cookie (HttpOnly). Add an `iat` check.
  - **Follow-ups as recorded: accurate.** `GET /api/session/clear` clears the cookie on a cross-site navigation (confirmed, 307 + expired Set-Cookie; the server session is not revoked) and so does a cross-site `POST step=logout` (also deletes the cookie without a CSRF/Origin check): nuisance logout only, LOW. Non-form POST to `/api/session` (JSON body or none) -> 500 with generic body (`Content-Type was not one of multipart/form-data...` logged server-side only; no leak): LOW, should be 400. A valid cookie with Appwrite 5xx/network error -> 500, not a loop (acceptable). Unauthenticated `next` value has no length cap (1 MB accepted, self-DoS only).
  - **Verified sound:** (1) `safeNext` against `//evil.com`, `/\evil.com`, `/%09/evil.com`, `/%0d%0a//evil.com`, `/%2f/`, `/%5c`, raw tab/CR/LF, `/..//evil.com`, `/.//`, `javascript:`, `data:`, single/double-encoded variants, fullwidth/division-slash/zero-width/BOM/NBSP lookalikes, `@`, 1 MB: every result resolves to the same origin (accepted `/..//evil.com` becomes a same-origin path `//evil.com`, not a network-path reference); login POST with these `next` values redirects only to `/login...` or `/`. (2) Built app with stubbed `GET /v1/account`: curator `/payments` -> 307 `/forbidden`, also with `RSC: 1`, `_rsc`, `Next-Router-Prefetch`, forged `Next-Router-State-Tree` (flight payload contains only `NEXT_REDIRECT;/forbidden`, no page text); no cookie -> `/login?next=`; spoofed `x-pathname` ignored; middleware path variants (`//payments`, `/Payments`, `%70ayments`, trailing slash, `/_next/data`) never reach content; direct POST with `Next-Action` header -> 404, plain POST to a page -> 307. (3) MFA: password-only session (stub `user_more_factors_required`), mfa=false admin, expired (401), no label, `OpsAdmin`, `ops_admin` all -> 307 `/api/session/clear`; good opsadmin/superadmin -> 200; pending-MFA cookie planted without session cookie -> login redirect; `hs_admin_mfa` only read by `POST /api/session`. (5) Hooks: from repo root `uvx pre-commit run --all-files` all pass after `npm ci` (prettier catches a badly formatted scratch file; eslint passes it, as it should).
  - **Commands:** `cd apps/admin && npm ci && npx tsc --noEmit && npm test && npm run lint && npm run build`: tsc ok, 99 passed (98+1 skipped before first build), lint clean, build clean. `make lint`: 5 ran ok, 5 skipped, 0 failed. `make test`: 6 ran ok, 3 skipped, **1 failed (scripts, F-1)**. No Appwrite API called, stub only.
- **F06 AUDIT-FIX BLOCK (26 Nov):** Status **audit-closed** (merged PR #167, 7f740cf). Reviewer: clean worktree rebased on main, tsc/124 tests (123+1 skipped)/lint/build, `make lint`, pre-commit green; built app vs stub /v1/account: role/RSC/prefetch/MFA matrix unchanged, superadmin matrix over all 16 label subsets consistent, tampered pending cookies rejected, cancel deletes the session, `/api/session/clear` leaves a valid admin alone, GET logout 405, no key in `.next/static`. Non-blocking follow-ups: (1) guard scanner accepts an early `return` before the guard and skips not-found/loading, metadata routes (sitemap, opengraph-image) and `src/pages`; (2) `isSameOriginPost` 403s behind a proxy sending a comma-list `X-Forwarded-Host` or rewriting Host without it, even with Sec-Fetch-Site same-origin (trust Sec-Fetch-Site first, take first XFH); redirects also use `req.url` host (check on deploy); (3) pending cookie has no explicit user id/purpose and no clock-skew tolerance; (4) SDK says `Session.secret` is returned only with an API key: verify real login on staging; (5) F09 inventory needs an `ADMIN_COOKIE_SECRET` row (F09 not merged yet). Layouts may use `currentAdmin()`. Public routes are the `PUBLIC_ROUTES` allowlist in the test, each with a reason (min 20 chars, stale entries fail): root layout, `login`, `(admin)/forbidden`, `api/session`, `api/session/clear`. | `tests/page-guard.test.ts` (16): real app clean; fixtures in temp trees fail for unguarded page outside `(admin)`, unguarded `route.ts` in `(admin)`, unguarded `route.ts` under `api/`, guard only in a comment (also only in a string), one-of-two methods, wrong route, `@slot`/`default`, layout, server actions. Also mutation on the real tree (`src/app/zzz/page.tsx` unguarded) -> test red. Middleware: now also checks the cookie has a plausible opaque-token shape (`isPlausibleSessionCookie`, 16-2048 chars, no whitespace/braces); real validity is per request via `requireAccess` |

### M01 · Core schema: users, roles, profiles, children, locations, consent (#36)
- Status: **blocked-foundation-merged**. PR #168 squash-merged (a17376b), issue #36 closed. Owner-blocked: apply 0002 to a non-live project, verify REST column shapes, source-of-truth choice (below). Reviewer round 2 below.
- (Builder's status line, kept:) was ready-for-review on branch `m01-core-schema` (commit starts `M01:`; branch pushed, PR not opened). Blocked on owner only for applying anywhere real (see below).
- **Schema convention (M02/M03/M04 follow this):** one JSON file per domain in `appwrite/schema/` (`_base.json` = F02 runner table + CI sample table, `core.json` = M01). A ticket edits ONLY its own domain file (add `catalogue.json`, `scheduling.json`...). `appwrite/schema_build.py` validates every table and **generates** (a) the `tables` array of `appwrite/appwrite.config.json` (other keys stay hand-edited, so F04 editing `functions` does not conflict) and (b) `docs/erd/<domain>.md` (Mermaid). `--check` (default; also in `packages.json` lint for the appwrite package, so `make lint`) fails when generated files are stale; `--write` regenerates. If a rebase conflicts in a generated file, take either side and rerun `--write`. Domain file shape: `tables` (Appwrite CLI table objects without databaseId), `meta[table].deletion` in soft|history|immutable|join|hard (+ `note`), `relations` (`from: table.column`, `to`, optional `deferred` when the target table arrives in a later ticket). Validator rules: rowSecurity true; table `$permissions` empty (no any/users, no create/update/delete); required column cannot have a default (Appwrite; this caught F02's `sample_item.status`, now optional with default); varchar 1..16383, indexed varchar sum <= 768 chars, no text index, <= 64 indexes, one array column per index, row width estimate <= 65,535 bytes; `*_at` is datetime (UTC) and datetimes end `_at`; calendar dates are varchar(10) ISO; coordinates integer micro-degrees (`*_e6`); money-named columns integer; no float types; role enum values alphanumeric; every FK is varchar(36), indexed (first index column) and resolves to a table; soft tables need optional `deleted_at`; immutable tables have no deleted_at/revoked_at/updated_at.
- **Applying:** `AppwriteClient.ensure_table(spec)` added to the F02 interface (non-abstract; convergent: creates missing table/columns/indexes, errors on drift, never alters). Implemented in `fake_client.py` (mirrors Appwrite checks) and `appwrite_client.py` (REST shapes from docs, NOT run live: `columns/varchar|text|integer|boolean|datetime|enum`, owner step: verify once on dev). `migrations/0002_core_schema.py` calls `schema_build.apply_domain(client, "core")`. Later schema changes = edit the domain JSON + new migration calling `apply_domain(client, domain, only=[...])` (0002 is never edited). No rows inserted, no seed.
- **Decisions (documented in `core.json` meta notes):** roles: Appwrite labels authoritative (no role column on `user_profile`; its `$id` = auth user id); `role_assignment` is the grant history with `active_key` (`user:role` while active, null after revoke) unique, so unique (user, role) among active grants and re-grant works. `consent_record` per child, insert-only, withdrawal = new row `decision=withdrawn`; `policy_acceptance` insert-only, unique (user, policy, version). Immutability is enforced by no client write permission + schema flag `immutable`; the core `Repository` (F03) has `update`/`delete` for every table and no per-table guard, so **follow-up for M05/M06**: core-service consent/policy repository wrapper exposing only `create`/`list`/`get` (test: update/delete raise), plus the permission-audit forbidding writes. `desired_permissions` NOT done (M05 #40). Child: no unique (parent, name, dob) (twins, re-adds): duplicates are a core-service warning; `school`/`class_level` optional; no photo (DP-3). `home_location`: several per parent, `default_key` (= parent_id on the one default row, null otherwise) unique, separate table from `child` (ADR 0012). `teacher_approval` is one row per (teacher, curriculum, level) (SR says `level_ids` array; rows are indexable/queryable). `teacher_zone` join table (SR "zones" field; hard delete allowed). Availability weekly pattern: ISO weekday 1-7, minutes since midnight Africa/Kampala wall-clock (recurring pattern, not an instant); exceptions are UTC instants. Soft delete (`deleted_at`) on all business tables except consent/policy (immutable), `role_assignment` (`revoked_at`) and `teacher_zone`. FKs to tables of later tickets (curriculum, catalogue_version, level, tier) are `deferred` relations (indexed already). `parent_profile.preferred_contact` (push/email/sms) is my minimal reading of "preferences".
- Files: `appwrite/schema/{_base,core}.json`, `appwrite/schema_build.py`, `appwrite/diff_live.py`, `appwrite/migrations/0002_core_schema.py`, `appwrite/tests/test_schema.py` (59 tests), edits to `appwrite/{migrate,fake_client,appwrite_client}.py`, generated `appwrite/appwrite.config.json` and `docs/erd/core.md`, `appwrite/README.md`, `packages.json` (appwrite lint adds schema_build/diff_live compile + `schema_build.py --check`).
- Test command: `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -v` -> Ran 141 OK (60 F02 + new). `python3 appwrite/schema_build.py --check`. Regenerate: `python3 appwrite/schema_build.py --write`. Root `make lint`: appwrite ok (admin lint fails only where `npm ci` was not run).

| Criterion / edge case | Test (`appwrite/tests/test_schema.py`) |
|---|---|
| Tables, FKs, indexes as SR 5.1 | `CoreTables.test_all_issue_tables_exist`, `test_sr_5_1_key_fields`, `test_every_foreign_key_is_indexed_and_resolves`, `test_every_id_like_column_is_declared_a_relation_or_deferred`, `test_lookup_indexes_the_issue_implies`, `test_unique_*`, validator negatives `test_validator_rejects_unindexed_foreign_key`, `..._relation_to_missing_table` |
| Money integer UGX; timestamps UTC | `test_timestamps_are_datetime_and_named_at`, `test_validator_rejects_non_datetime_at_column`, `test_no_float_columns_and_money_columns_must_be_integer`, `test_dates_without_time_are_iso_strings_and_coordinates_integers` (no money columns exist in M01) |

- **Live diff (owner decision 2; snapshot read-only, not committed; names only), M01 entities** (`python3 appwrite/diff_live.py <snapshot> --alias role_assignment=user_role`):
  - Match in table + columns (differences only noted below): `availability_slot`, `availability_exception`, `zone`, `teacher_zone`, `role_assignment` (live name `user_role`). Live has no column `deleted_at` on availability_slot, availability_exception, zone, teacher_document, teacher_approval, teacher_profile (repo adds it per SR 5.5). Recommendation: keep repo (`deleted_at`).
  - Table-level permissions: live tables carry `read(label:opsadmin)`/`read(label:superadmin)` (some also `safeguardinglead`) at TABLE level; repo has none (ADR 0012 default deny, per-row grants; superadmin has no data read). Recommendation: keep repo; live table-level label read also contradicts ADR 0012 item 3 and SR 6.2 (safeguarding lead scoped to cases).
  - Added after review round 1 (names only): `teacher_zone` also differs live by a `pair_key` column (live uses it for pair uniqueness; repo uses a composite unique index); `policy_acceptance.app` is a required column live (repo has no `app` column; decide with the policies ticket whether acceptance records which app it was given in).
  - Repo tables absent live: `parent_profile`, `guardian_link`, `home_location`, `child`, `consent_record`. Live tables absent from repo (outside M01 or later tickets): the other 25 (catalogue, ledger, notifications, jobs, settings, audit, referees, policy_document/version...). Recommendation: create the 5 new ones; keep the 25 live-only ones as input to the tickets that own them.
  - `user_profile`: live has `first_name`, `last_name` (repo: single `name`), `deleted_at` also in live (matches); live `status` enum has extra member `deleted`; live `email` is string with email format (repo varchar(254), unique index on email and phone; live has non-unique indexes). Recommendation: take live `first_name`/`last_name` (better for greetings; costs nothing), keep unique phone/email, drop the `deleted` status member in favour of `deleted_at` (single source of truth).
  - `role_assignment`: live `user_role` has the same design (`user_id`, `role`, `granted_by_id`, `granted_at`, `revoked_at`, `revoke_reason`, `active_key` unique). Repo has two more indexes (`granted_by_id`, `role`+`revoked_at`). Recommendation: adopt the name `user_role`? Repo follows the issue name `role_assignment`; pick one (default: keep `role_assignment`, rename live later).
  - `policy_acceptance`: live uses `policy_version_id` (FK to live-only `policy_version`), `policy_key`, `signature_name`, required `app`; repo uses `policy` + `version`. Recommendation: merge: adopt live's `policy_version` table design when the policies ticket arrives (D06/legal), keep `signature_name`.
  - `teacher_approval`: live row per (teacher, curriculum, level) like repo, but with `subject_id`, `applied`, `approved`, `approval_key` (unique) and indexes (teacher_id; curriculum+level+approved); repo uses a composite unique index instead of `approval_key`. Recommendation: merge: add `approved` (approved vs merely applied is needed by VER-1) and `subject_id` if subjects gate approval; composite unique replaces `approval_key`.
  - `teacher_document`: live has `uploaded_at`, `part`, `superseded_by_id`, `current_key` (unique; supports re-upload history); repo has none. Recommendation: adopt live (re-uploads must keep history); repo adds an `expiry_date` index (daily job) and `verified_by_id` index.
  - `teacher_profile`: live has many application fields (`title`, `date_of_birth`, `languages`, `teacher_type`, `registration_number`, `highest_qualification`, `institution`, `years_teaching`, `current_school`, `application_status`, `interview_at`, `interview_location`, `submitted_at`, `max_travel_minutes`) and no `user_id`/`tier_id`/`travel_radius_km`; repo has `user_id` (unique), `tier_id`, `travel_radius_km`, `deleted_at`, and none of the application fields. Recommendation: merge: take live's application fields (VER-1 needs them) in M-ticket for teacher onboarding; use `max_travel_minutes` (matching works in minutes) instead of km; use row `$id` = user id instead of a `user_id` column if the owner prefers (live way), or keep `user_id`.
  - Index design differences: live uses `*_key` helper columns (`pair_key`, `approval_key`, `active_key`, `current_key`) for uniqueness; repo uses real composite unique indexes where all columns are required, and `*_key` only for partial uniqueness (active grants, single default home, active guardian link).
  - Recommendation overall: **merge**, taking the repo as base (ADR 0012 compliant permissions, SR 5.1 entities complete, validated limits) and absorbing live's richer teacher/policy/document fields. Do not apply anything to live; changes to live go through a staging project first.

Own checks: drift (type/size/required/array/default/elements/min/max; null vs missing default equal; permission order ignored, duplicate permission and rowSecurity flip/missing flagged; index type/columns flagged); stuck and failed fail fast, timeout 300 s, partial resume (table + columns, no indexes) converges; validate() flags undeclared owner_id and parent_ids in a scratch domain. - **Non-blocking follow-ups from round 2 (for M05/M06 and M02-M04 convention):** (1) Soft delete vs unique indexes: soft-deleted rows keep their unique values, so `user_profile.phone/email`, `parent_profile.user_id`, `teacher_profile.user_id`, `zone.name`, `teacher_approval` (teacher, curriculum, level) and `policy_acceptance` block re-creation until the row is restored (clear `deleted_at`) or the retention job nulls/removes the value; document the rule (or use the `active_key` pattern, null when deleted) in the convention. Unique indexes allow multiple NULLs (MariaDB), so `active_key`/`default_key` work. (2) `non_fk` reason is not checked for empty text. (3) Drift compare: enum elements are order-sensitive, integer min/max returned as strings would be a false drift, an existing index in `failed` status is not rechecked by `ensure_table` (runner wait covers new ones); verify when the owner runs the real REST check. (4) Enum or column changes need a new migration (ensure_table never alters).

### M02 · Schema: plans, subscriptions, matching, assignments, sessions (#37)
- Status: **merged** (PR #182, squash; migration 0003 stays with M02, M04 rebases on it and renumbers to 0004+).

Re-attacks: restore with a taken value -> `UniqueViolation`; free value -> keys rebuilt; 200 threaded restore-vs-create races always leave exactly one active row; restore of a never-deleted row is idempotent; cancelled session restored stays released; no table has more than one derived key. Mutations of `SOFT_UNIQUE_LEGACY` (add sessions pair, add real core pair, remove one) all fail the pin tests. Zero/negative/equal/tz-equivalent ends rejected in `create_session` and `substitute_session_row`; adjacent allowed; mixed timezones compared correctly. Issues #93/#97/#134/#40 notes appended after the original bodies; questions 19-20 intact.
- **Non-blocking follow-ups (round 2, for W01 #97/S01 #93 port or a chore):** (1) the lint-style `deleted_at` scan is a regex for the literal forms `"deleted_at": None` and `deleted_at=None` in .py files only; it is evaded by `p["deleted_at"] = None`, a variable or concatenated key, `[("deleted_at", None)]`, a value held in a variable, and non-Python code. It is a useful tripwire, not proof; the README/test should say so, and the real guard is review. (2) Naive datetimes are accepted and treated as UTC by `parse_utc` (not rejected); `None` start/end raises `AttributeError` rather than `InvalidSession`. W01 should validate input at the API boundary (timezone-aware ISO only). (3) A second soft-delete overwrites `deleted_at` (harmless). (4) `soft_restore_patch` trusts the caller to pass the matching `with_*_keys`; core tables (role_assignment, guardian_link, home_location) have no `with_*_keys` helper in the repo yet.
- (Previous status line: ready-for-review (round 2), head 58c938c.)
- **Round 2 resolutions (builder):**
  1. Restore: `schema_build.soft_restore_patch(row, with_keys)` (mirror of `soft_delete_patch`; recomputes every `*_key` through the table's `with_*_keys`); README rule "restore = recompute keys, 409 if the value is taken; a bare `deleted_at: None` patch is forbidden for ALL domains". Tests: `SoftDeleteUniqueRule.test_restore_recomputes_keys_and_conflicts_with_a_newer_active_row` (A1 soft-deleted, A2 created, restore A1 -> `UniqueViolation`; the bare patch is shown to produce two active rows), `test_restore_without_conflict_rebuilds_keys_for_every_table`, lint-style `test_no_code_clears_deleted_at_without_the_restore_helper` (scans appwrite/ and services/core, excluding tests and schema_build).
  2. `SOFT_UNIQUE_LEGACY` pinned: `test_legacy_exemptions_are_pinned_to_the_exact_six` (literal set; adding needs an owner decision), `test_legacy_exemptions_are_minimal_core_soft_unique_indexes` (each pair is a core soft-table unique index, exactly those pairs violate without the exemption, none in the sessions domain; `soft_unique_violations(domains, legacy=...)` takes the set as a parameter). Follow-up owner: dedicated chore **`chore-soft-unique-core`** (derived `*_key` columns + migration 0004+ + backfill for the six core unique indexes), to run before M05/M06 consume those tables or be folded into M05 #40 (append-only note added to #40).

- **Findings to fix (small; tests/docs/helper, no schema change):**
  1. **Restore bypasses the unique rule (convention hole for ALL domains).** Soft delete nulls the keys, but restoring (clearing `deleted_at`) without recomputing them leaves `active_key` null: repro: soft-delete assignment A1, create A2 with the same pair, restore A1 with `{"deleted_at": None}` succeeds, two active assignments. Add a documented rule plus helper (e.g. `schema_build.restore_requires_key_rebuild` / per-table `restore_*_patch` in `session_rules.py` that rebuilds via `with_*_keys` and so raises `UniqueViolation` on conflict) and a test; README "Soft delete and unique values" must say "restore = recompute keys, 409 if the value is taken".
  2. **`SOFT_UNIQUE_LEGACY` can grow silently.** `test_core_exemptions_are_explicit_and_exist` only checks non-empty and subset of existing M01 indexes, so adding any (table, index) passes. Pin the exact six pairs in a test (assertEqual to a literal set, with a comment that adding needs an owner decision) and add a minimality test: each listed pair must be an M01 (`core`) soft-table unique index that WITHOUT the exemption yields a violation, and no sessions-domain pair may be listed. Also name an owner for the follow-up: suggest a dedicated chore `chore-soft-unique-core` (derived `*_key` columns + migration 0004+ + backfill) to be done before M05/M06 consume those tables, or fold into M05 #40; add it to the M02 follow-ups.
  3. **`create_session` accepts `scheduled_end_at <= scheduled_start_at`** (repro: start=07:00 end=06:00 inserted; zero-length same-start does not overlap an existing one). Reject end <= start (new `InvalidSession`/ValueError) with a test; W01/S01 port it.
  4. **Residual overlap risk is not in the README Sessions section** (only "backstop unique_teacher_slot"). Add one sentence: same-start only; different-start overlaps need per-teacher serialisation (W01 #97 / S01 #93). The reviewer already appended this note (plus end>start and restore-recompute) to issues #97 and #93.

- **Built:** `appwrite/schema/sessions.json` (10 tables: plan, subscription, match_request, match_offer, assignment, session, session_checkin, session_confirmation, cancellation, dispute), migration `appwrite/migrations/0003_sessions_schema.py` (rebase onto main: renumber to the next free version if another schema ticket took 0003; the runner rejects duplicate versions), `appwrite/session_rules.py` (reference module for W01 #97: SR 7.4 `ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS` as data, derived-key builders, overlap predicate, `create_session` = service overlap check then insert with the unique slot index as backstop, substitute row builder, late/on-time cancellation), fake client row API with unique-index semantics (`create_row/update_row/list_rows`, NULLs never collide, `UniqueViolation`, thread-safe), generated `appwrite.config.json` tables and `docs/erd/sessions.md`, README sections. Existing M01 migration tests loosened to `applied[:2] == [1, 2]` so later schema tickets do not break them.
- **Soft-delete vs unique rule (decision, convention for ALL domains; `appwrite/README.md`):** on a `soft` table every unique index is over derived optional `*_key` columns that the core service sets to null on soft delete (`schema_build.soft_delete_patch`) and when the row stops holding the value. Enforced by `schema_build.soft_unique_violations` inside `validate()`. M01 tables not yet compliant, exempted explicitly in `schema_build.SOFT_UNIQUE_LEGACY` (core.json untouched): `user_profile` (`unique_phone`, `unique_email`), `parent_profile.unique_user`, `teacher_profile.unique_user`, `zone.unique_name`, `teacher_approval.unique_teacher_curriculum_level`. Follow-up ticket: derived `*_key` columns + migration for them. (`guardian_link`, `home_location`, `role_assignment` already comply.)
- **Decisions:** unique constraints via derived keys: `match_offer.active_key` (`request:teacher` while pending), `assignment.active_key` (`child:subscription` while end_at null; a trial counts, convert = end with `trial_converted` then create), `session.teacher_slot_key` (`teacher|UTC start` while the session holds the slot; cancelled_*/no_show/void release it) , `dispute.open_key`, `plan.active_key`. **Deviation from the ticket wording:** the unique backstop is on the derived `teacher_slot_key` instead of raw (teacher_id, scheduled_start_at), so a cancelled session does not block re-booking and the soft-delete rule holds. Substitute = a new session row (`is_substitute`, `substitute_for_session_id`, `assignment_id` null). `rank_score` integer basis points, `score_breakdown` JSON text. Check-in coordinates `lat_e6/lng_e6`. Immutable: session_checkin (unique session+type), session_confirmation, cancellation (unique session). `plan.price_ugx` integer UGX + `currency`. Session status transitions are enforced by the core service (table in `session_rules.py`; the schema only restricts the enum). Not added: unique one-subscription-per-child, single pending offer per request (service rules).
- **Residual risk (documented in `session_rules.py`):** the backstop catches two requests with the SAME start; two concurrent requests with different but overlapping starts can both pass the service check. W01 must serialise writes per teacher (one writer per teacher key) or re-check after insert. Appwrite transactions are not relied on.

(2) `Runner._apply` should re-read `job_run` and skip if terminal/superseded: a stalled claimer's late failure flips a completed run to `queued` transiently (self-heals via attempt-row reconcile, handler not re-run). (3) A corrupt `state:<job>` payload raises out of `tick()` for ALL jobs; a future watermark silently stalls one job: isolate per job and alert. (4) Runs of a job removed from `schedule.py` stay queued and can fill the 50-row claim window: mark them skipped. (5) State rows (`status=skipped`, `dedupe_key=state:*`) show in an unfiltered admin list: M06 must filter (or move to a `job_state` table). (6) `job_run`/`job_attempt` are NOT in the schema build (M01 domain files): add a `jobs.json` domain with the columns incl. `chunk`, `generation`, `result`, `outcome`, `finished_at`, `error`, `items` and index `by_status_run_at`; re-verify read-only against live. (7) ADR 0002 must be updated by a decision-owner PR: 15 s tick budget (not 20 s), attempt row as source of truth, streak counts failed attempts (failed ticks that never reach the core need Appwrite execution-failure alerts or the F08 heartbeat), test table incl. `test_signed_tick_runs_due_jobs`. (8) After F08 merges: ping `HS_HEARTBEAT_URL` and tag job failures `alert=job-failure`. (9) Function: validate response shape / do not follow redirects; endpoint reads the body before auth when no Content-Length (cap via proxy). (10) Limits to document: worker clock skew above ~5 min can trigger early lease takeover; timed-out handler threads cannot be killed (effects must use `ctx.effect_key`); permanently failing job grows one run + up to max_attempts attempt rows per period (needs a retention job).
- **Original builder block follows.** ready-for-review. Branch `f04-job-scheduler` (commit starts `F04:`, rebased on main at 74e7de6, pushed, PR not opened). Foundation only: expect `blocked-foundation-merged`.
- **Built:** `services/core/src/hs_core/jobs/`: `schedule.py` (the ONE list, `JOBS`, Kampala-anchored `Schedule`; sample `heartbeat` every 5 min), `model.py` (`Done`/`Continue`/`JobContext`/`JobSpec`/`Schedule`), `runner.py`, `idempotency.py` (`dedupe_key(name, period)`, deterministic row IDs), `alerts.py` (`AlertSink`, `FakeAlertSink`, default `LogAlertSink`), `signing.py`, `api.py` (`POST /internal/jobs/tick`), `handlers.py`. Wired in `main.create_app(..., runner=, alert_sink=)`. Function `appwrite/functions/job-tick/src/main.py` (stdlib only, 25 s HTTP timeout, https only, no secret logged) and its entry in `appwrite/appwrite.config.json` (python-3.12, `* * * * *`, timeout 30 s, no execute/events/scopes). Settings: `JOB_TICK_SECRET` (required >=32 chars in staging/production), `JOB_ALERT_AFTER_FAILURES` (3), `APPWRITE_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_S`/`APPWRITE_READ_TIMEOUT_S`. Repository: typed `Conflict` (409; still a `ValueError`), thread-safe `InMemoryRepository` with Appwrite-style `queries` (equal/compare/order/limit/offset), `install_request_timeout`.
- **Design (vs the spike):** attempt rows (`job_attempt`: run_id, chunk, attempt, generation, started_at, outcome, finished_at, error, items, result) are the source of truth and are written BEFORE `job_run`, so a crash in between is reconciled from them without re-running the handler. A claimer checks it still holds the claim (own lease not within 30 s of expiry, no later generation row) before AND after the `job_run` update; a claimer that stalled past the lease stands down. `locked_until` is computed at write time so a late clobber is benign. Watermark and failure streak live in one non-claimable per-job `job_run` row (`dedupe_key=state:<job>`, status `skipped`). Claimable query = `status` + `run_at` (index `by_status_run_at`), `order_asc(run_at)`, `limit(claim_batch=50)`, queued and running as two queries; lease expiry filtered in code. Backoff `base*2^(n-1)` capped at 1 h. Handler timeout (default 10 s) runs the handler in a thread; on timeout the attempt fails, `ctx.cancelled` is set (the thread cannot be killed: handlers must be idempotent by `ctx.effect_key`). Alerts: `final_failure` once per run, `consecutive_failures` once when the streak of failed attempts reaches N (resets on success). Alert sink errors are swallowed.
- **Deviations / notes for the reviewer:** (1) tick budget 15 s and job timeout 10 s (ADR text says 20 s budget) so budget + timeout stays under the function's 25 s HTTP timeout; ADR 0002 wording should follow. (2) "consecutive failed runs" is implemented as consecutive failed attempts per job; whole failed ticks that never reach the core (function or network down) are not visible to the core: alert on those via Appwrite execution failures or a Sentry cron/heartbeat monitor (F08). (3) Per-job state rows are stored in `job_run` to avoid a new table; M-ticket owners may prefer a `job_state` table. (4) Extra `job_attempt` columns `chunk`, `generation`, `result` besides ADR follow-up `outcome`, `finished_at`, `error` (+ `items`): F02/M schema must create them; the live `job_run`/`job_attempt` columns and the `by_status_run_at` index are still unverified against the F02 build (no job tables in `appwrite.config.json` yet). (5) `spikes/jobs-demo` left in place. (6) ADR 0002's test table still omits the spike's `test_signed_tick_runs_due_jobs`; not edited here. (7) Function variables are not in the CLI config: set `CORE_SERVICE_URL` and `JOB_TICK_SECRET` in Appwrite per environment. (8) F03 follow-up 5 (`/ready` key scope) not done; F03 follow-up 4 repeated-placeholder key check not done (only the localhost part, as asked).

- **Reviewer findings (round 1, 25 Nov 2026).** Ran in a fresh worktree after rebasing onto origin/main (clean; only memory commits had moved; rebased commit NOT pushed since the branch needs changes anyway, builder rebases when fixing): `cd appwrite && python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` Ran 119 OK; `schema_build.py --check` ok (17 tables); `make lint` 5 ok / 0 failed; `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests` ok; `make test` and `uvx pre-commit run --all-files` started but not awaited to the end (long; reviewer relies on package-level runs above). Mutation checks all fail as they should: `read("any")`/`read("users")`/table label read, rowSecurity false, float money/lat, varchar lat, varchar >16383, row width, underscore role label, FK without index, datetime not `_at`, `_at` not datetime, soft without deleted_at, index > 768 chars, stale config, stale ERD. Idempotency on a fresh fake: 15 tables/98 columns, rerun is a no-op; half-created table converges on rerun; column type drift raises. No snapshot content or live IDs in the branch diff (grepped). Live diff summary checked against the snapshot with `diff_live.py`: accurate (minor: `teacher_zone` also differs live by `pair_key`; `policy_acceptance.app` is required live). Schema vs SR 5.1/5.5 and issue #36: all entities and acceptance criteria present; design decisions are sound (active_key uniqueness, default_key, per-child consent with withdrawal rows, micro-degree coordinates are fine, ADR 0012 address visibility is a core-service/permission concern and the separate `home_location` table supports it). Defects to fix (then re-hand-off; keep it small):
  1. **`ensure_table` creates indexes right after columns with no wait** (`appwrite_client.py`, and the fake does not model it). Appwrite rejects an index on a column still `processing`, so 0002 would very likely fail on a real project (the headline "applies to a fresh project"). Fix: after adding columns and before creating indexes, wait until the table's columns are `available` (use the same sleep/now/timeout injection as `migrate.wait_until_available`, or have `apply_domain` call it per table); make `FakeAppwriteClient.ensure_table` reject an index on a column whose status is still `processing` (so a test with `polls_until_available>0` proves the wait). Also the real adapter's drift check compares only `required`; compare `type`/`size`/`array`/enum `elements` like the fake (map the REST `type` + `format` shape), and add a test with a canned REST response.
  2. **Validator gap for the convention M02-M04 will copy:** "every `*_id` column must be a declared relation (so its FK index is enforced)" is only a test on the core domain (`test_every_id_like_column_is_declared_a_relation_or_deferred`). A new domain table with an undeclared `owner_id` passes `validate()` (reproduced). Move the rule into `validate()` (allow-list for file ids, e.g. `file_id`, `photo_file_id`, or a `file` marker), keep a negative test.
  3. **Insert-only claim is overstated in the schema notes.** `core.json` meta for `consent_record` says "the core service exposes no update or delete for it", which is not true today (F03 `Repository` has update/delete for every table). Reword in `core.json` (and regenerate ERD) to: no client write permission now; core-service guard is a follow-up (M05/M06). Also state in the notes (and `appwrite/README.md` convention section) how `immutable`/`soft` tables meet DP-6/SR 5.5 ("hard delete only under the retention policy"): the retention job is the only allowed hard-deleter (child consent rows are removed with the child under the retention policy), so M05/M06 must make that explicit exception. Docs only.
  4. **Drift semantics, document or detect:** `ensure_table` ignores differing index definitions (same key, different columns/type) and table `rowSecurity`/`$permissions` drift, in both fake and real adapter. Either raise on them or document "index and permission drift is not detected" in the `ensure_table` docstring and README. Also note in `availability_slot` meta that overlapping slots are allowed by the schema and de-duplicated/merged by the core service (the edge case is otherwise silent).
  Owner-blocked and accepted as-is: applying 0002 to a non-live project, live REST shape verification, source-of-truth choice, repository-level insert-only guard as a clearly stated M05/M06 follow-up. The `sample_item.status` change (required column with a default is invalid in Appwrite) is justified.

- **Round-2 review (resolved in round 3 above):** changes-requested (reviewer round 2; branch `s02-momo-package` rebased onto main as ec3aae6 (force-with-lease pushed), no PR). Round-1 findings 1-5 are verified RESOLVED. Two NEW blocking findings, found by reviewer repros (worktree removed):
  1. (blocking, security + money) `UrllibTransport` auto-follows redirects. Repro with two localhost servers: origin answers 301/302/303 -> urllib re-issues a GET to the other host WITH `Authorization: Bearer ...` (and the Basic API key on the token call) and `Ocp-Apim-Subscription-Key`, then returns the other host's 200 as the response. A POST redirected this way comes back as HTTP 200 = "accepted" (client treats 200 as accepted) although MTN never created anything; GET 307/308 also follow. (POST 307/308 are not followed; they come back as 3xx and are probed correctly.) Fix: install a no-redirect opener in `UrllibTransport` (HTTPRedirectHandler whose `redirect_request` returns None so 3xx come back as responses), and add tests against a localhost http.server pair asserting the second server is never contacted for 301/302/303/307/308 on GET and POST and that the 3xx surfaces (POST 3xx -> probed, GET 3xx -> not a result). Also treat a 200 on POST as accepted only when it is not from a redirect (covered by the no-follow fix).
  2. (blocking) `Retry-After` is not sanitised: `float()` accepts `-5`, `nan`, `-inf`; `_backoff` then calls `time.sleep` with it and raises a raw `ValueError`/`OverflowError` (repro with the real `time.sleep`: POST 429 with `Retry-After: -5|nan|-inf` -> `ValueError`/`OverflowError` escapes `request_to_pay` instead of `RateLimited`; same from `get_status`). Huge/`inf`/`1e999` values are capped at `backoff_max_seconds` correctly; dates/garbage/empty fall back to normal backoff. Fix: parse to a finite float, clamp to `[0, backoff_max_seconds]` (reject non-finite/negative -> None), also store the clamped value in `RateLimited.retry_after`; tests for `-5`, `nan`, `-inf`, `inf`, `1e999`, date, garbage using the real clamp (assert `_sleep` argument is finite and within range).
  Nits (no change required for merge): README "Test command" and memory.md "How to run tests" still say 130 tests (now 215). The 429-on-POST-means-not-accepted assumption is documented and accepted.

Callback fail-closed cases (wrong/missing/fractional/bool amount, wrong currency, unknown ref, URL/body ref mismatch, non-JSON/list/null/non-UTF8, status endpoint down/timeout, FAILED vs body SUCCESSFUL, replay x3) all behave (reject, raise, or use MTN's answer). (blocking) `_create` re-POSTs (same reference) when the status query is inconclusive (5xx/timeout/429), contradicting the ticket ("never resend blindly") and the README/docstring ("re-POST only on definite 404"). Repro: timeout_before + GET timeouts forever -> 4 POSTs. Re-POST only after a definite 404 from status; otherwise raise `OutcomeUnknown`. Add a test asserting POST count stays 1 when status is 5xx/timeout.
  2. (blocking) After a POST that may have been accepted, `_create` leaks other exceptions: status 401x2 -> `AuthError`, 403/400 -> `RequestRejected`, 5xx on the status or 409 path -> `ServerError`, status 404 after a 409 -> `NotFound` (all repro'd; payment WAS created). A consumer treating AuthError/RequestRejected as permanent failure and minting a new reference double-pays. Once a POST was sent, only a `PaymentResult`, `OutcomeUnknown` or a definite 4xx POST rejection may escape; wrap the rest as `OutcomeUnknown`. Same for `get_status` inside the 409 branch. Tests for each.
